Build a Search Desk feature

Everything you need is below. There is nothing to install.

  1. Press Copy this kit under the one you want.
  2. Paste it into a free AI chat, in a new chat.
  3. Save the file it gives you as features/<id>.js. Plain text, not a word-processor file.
  4. Open features/list.js and add the id to Desk.features.
  5. Reload Search Desk and put #selftest at the end of the address.

Bigger text S

Larger text and stronger contrast, remembered on this device.

the kit as a file the brief

Add to calendar S

Put an interview or a starred event into your phone's calendar.

the kit as a file the brief

Posting checklist S

What a posting must tell you in Ontario, and whether this one does.

the kit as a file the brief

People I met M

A short note about people you met, linked to an event or a job.

the kit as a file the brief

Pay notes S

The pay each posting gives, beside the pay you are aiming for.

the kit as a file the brief

One-page summary S

A page you can print or save as a PDF: this week's jobs, dates and events.

the kit as a file the brief

AI: follow-up message S

Drafts a short follow-up or thank-you note for a job. You copy it yourself.

the kit as a file the brief

AI: sites for my field S

Suggests job sites and organisations for your kind of work, as suggestions to add to `sites`.

the kit as a file the brief

AI: weekly check-in M

Sends your open jobs and suggests the next step for each, as suggestions.

the kit as a file the brief

Reply due S

After an interview, shows the date the employer should have told you the result by.

the kit as a file the brief

Which resume S

Which version of your resume you sent where.

the kit as a file the brief

Job sites S

Keep the job sites you use in one place, with a note about each.

the kit as a file the brief

My examples M

Short stories from your work, ready for interview questions; the coach recipe can include them.

the kit as a file the brief

This week S

How many you applied to, followed up and went to this week. No streaks and no scores.

the kit as a file the brief
